7.13          Insurance. From and after the Closing Date, the Companies shall cease to be insured by, have access or availability to, be entitled to make claims on, be entitled to claim benefits from or seek coverage under any policy of or agreement for insurance and interests in insurance pools and programs covering risks of Seller or its Affiliates (in each case including self-insurance and insurance from such an Affiliate) (“Seller Insurance”) and all rights of any nature with respect to any of the foregoing, including in each case all recoveries thereunder and rights to assert claims seeking any such recoveries. For any loss occurrence taking place prior to the Closing for which the Companies have claimed benefit, sought coverage or received payment under any Seller Insurance, and such loss has not been settled and discharged between the Companies and Seller on or prior to the Closing, the Companies shall reimburse Seller within thirty (30) days of receipt of an invoice for any costs, charges, retention, deductible, retrospective premium, cash payment for reserves calculated or charged on an incurred loss basis or other similar items and losses (including associated administrative expenses and allocated loss adjustment or similar expenses) incurred by or allocated to Seller. In the event that Buyer or its Affiliates (including, after the Closing, the Companies) receives any payment under any Seller Insurance on behalf of any business of Seller or any of its Affiliates after the Closing Date, such payments shall be the property of, and shall be forwarded and remitted to, Seller as promptly as practicable, but not later than twenty (20) days, after receipt thereof (or, if later, after notice or determination of receipt of such improper payment). Notwithstanding anything to the contrary contained herein, Buyer acknowledges that Seller is not responsible for procuring or maintaining insurance coverage with respect to the post-Closing conduct or activities of the Companies or the businesses, assets or properties of the Companies, including with respect to any Undisclosed Contracts.

 

7.14          Lock-Up.

 

(a)            Subject to Section 7.14(b) and Section 7.14(c), during the period commencing on the Closing Date and ending at 11:59 p.m. Eastern Time on the date that is twelve (12) months after the Closing Date (the “Lock-Up Period”), Seller shall not directly or indirectly sell or transfer any Acquirer Shares issued and delivered pursuant to Section 2.3(a) that are then held by Seller (the “Restricted Shares”); provided that nothing in this Section 7.14 shall restrict Seller from (i) entering into, establishing, maintaining or otherwise engaging in any hedging or other similar transaction or arrangement (including swaps, collars, put/call positions, forward or other derivative transactions) with respect to the Restricted Shares, (ii) pledging Restricted Shares as collateral to secure any obligations under, or in connection with, any such bona fide hedging transaction, or in a margin or similar account or (iii) taking any action to establish any of the foregoing, in each case so long as settlement of any such transaction, position or pledge does not require the sale or transfer of any Restricted Shares during the Lock-Up Period.

 

(b)            Notwithstanding anything in Section 7.14(a) to the contrary, Seller may transfer Restricted Shares (i) to one or more Affiliates, or (ii) pursuant to a Governmental Order or by operation of Law, provided that, in each case, each transferee agrees in writing to be bound by the restrictions applicable to Seller set forth in Section 7.14(a) for the remainder of the Lock-Up Period, in each case subject to the exceptions set forth in this Section 7.14(b) and Section 7.14(c).

(c)            Notwithstanding the foregoing, the restrictions in Section 7.14(a) shall not apply to any sale, transfer or exchange made pursuant to or in connection with any merger, tender offer, reclassification, reorganization, recapitalization, consolidation, stock exchange or similar transaction involving the Acquirer Shares made to all holders of Acquirer Shares that would result in the holders of Acquirer Shares exchanging their shares for cash, securities or other property.

 

7.15         Additional Listing Application; Removal of Restrictive Legends.

 

(a)            As promptly as practicable after the date of this Agreement, but in any event after taking into consideration the rules and regulations of the NYSE with respect to the timing of the Additional Listing Application (as hereinafter defined), Buyer shall submit to the NYSE an additional supplemental listing application relating to the Acquirer Shares to be issued and delivered pursuant to Section 2.3(a) (the “Additional Listing Application”) and shall use its commercially reasonable efforts to secure the NYSE’s approval of the Additional Listing Application, subject only to official notice of issuance.

 

(b)           Subject to compliance with the requirements set forth herein, upon the written request of Seller, in connection with any sale, assignment, transfer or other disposition of Acquirer Shares by Seller pursuant to Rule 144 or pursuant to any other exemption under the Securities Act such that upon such sale, assignment, transfer or other disposition the Acquirer Shares held by Seller become freely tradable, if requested by Seller, Buyer shall, at its own expense, promptly take or cause to be taken all actions reasonably necessary to cause any restrictive legends or similar notations imposed under the Securities Act or other applicable securities Laws to be removed from any certificate(s) or book-entry statements representing such Acquirer Shares held by Seller.

 

7.16         Wrong Pockets. In the event that Seller or any of its Affiliates receives any payments on behalf of the Companies (or any of them) with respect to the operation of their respective businesses after the Closing Date, such payments shall be the property of, and shall be forwarded and remitted to, Buyer or its designee as promptly as practicable, but no later than twenty (20) days, after receipt thereof (or, if later, after notice or determination of receipt of such improper payment). In the event that Buyer or its Affiliates (including, after the Closing, the Companies) receives any payments on behalf of any business of Seller or any of its Affiliates with respect to the operation of their respective businesses after the Closing Date, such payments shall be the property of, and shall be forwarded and remitted to, Seller as promptly as practicable, but not later than twenty (20) days, after receipt thereof (or, if later, after notice or determination of receipt of such improper payment).

 

7.17         Removal of Confidential Information. Promptly after Closing, Buyer shall use commercially reasonable efforts to, and shall cause the Companies and their Subsidiaries to use commercially reasonable efforts to, (a) conduct a reasonable and good-faith search of all information technology systems, servers, databases, shared drives, cloud storage environments, email systems, and other electronic repositories (collectively, the “Target Systems”) to identify any and all Confidential Information of Seller residing therein, excluding any Confidential Information that (i) Buyer or any of its Subsidiaries is entitled to access under any of the Related Agreement or (ii) is required to be retained by applicable Law in accordance with the terms of the confidentiality obligations under the Restrictive Covenant Agreement, and (b) permanently delete, destroy, or render unrecoverable all such Confidential Information from the Target Systems, in each case using commercially reasonable, industry-standard data deletion methods. Buyer shall direct all of the employees, agents, and subcontractors of the Companies and their Subsidiaries who had access to Seller’s Confidential Information to similarly delete and destroy all such information in their possession or control. Seller shall reasonably cooperate with Buyer to identify Seller Confidential Information residing on the Target Systems, including by designating a representative to assist Buyer in distinguishing Seller Confidential Information from information properly relating to the businesses of the Companies and their Subsidiaries. Buyer shall provide Seller and its designated representatives with reasonable access to the Target Systems (on a read-only basis and subject to reasonable confidentiality and security protocols) solely for purposes of facilitating such identification and verifying deletion in accordance with this Section 7.17.

Purchaser shall not, and shall cause its Affiliates (including the Companies) not to, contest the ownership, enforceability or validity of any rights of Seller or its Affiliates in or to any of the Seller Names.

The obligations of the Parties under this Agreement to consummate the transactions contemplated hereby and by the Related Agreements are subject to the satisfaction or, to the extent permitted by applicable Law, waiver by the Parties, of each of the following conditions as of the Closing Date:

 

(a)            Governmental Approvals. (b)            No Prohibition. No Governmental Order shall have been adopted, promulgated or entered by any Governmental Authority that is in effect and that prohibits the consummation of the transactions contemplated hereby.

 

8.2            Buyer Conditions. The obligations of Buyer under this Agreement to consummate the transactions contemplated by this Agreement are subject to the satisfaction (or waiver by Buyer) of each of the following conditions precedent:

 

(a)            Representations and Warranties True as of Closing.

(ii)            The representations and warranties of the Companies contained in ARTICLE 4 (without regard to any reference or qualifications to Material Adverse Effect or materiality) shall be true and correct, except where the failure of such representations and warranties to be so true and correct would not have a Material Adverse Effect, as of the Closing, as if made as of the Closing (except that the accuracy of representations and warranties that by their terms speak as of a specified date will be determined as of such date); provided that the representations and warranties set forth in Sections 4.1(a), 4.1(b), 4.2, 4.3(a)(i), 4.3(a)(ii), 4.16 and 4.20 shall be true and correct in all material respects as of the Closing, as if made as of the Closing (except that the accuracy of representations and warranties that by their terms speak as of a specified date will be determined as of such date).

(c)            No Material Adverse Effect. Since the date of this Agreement, there has not been any Material Adverse Effect that is continuing.

 

(d)            Target Cash Amount. Cash shall not be less than the Target Cash Amount.

ARTICLE 9

 

Termination

 

9.1           Termination. This Agreement may be terminated at any time on or prior to the Closing Date, as follows:

 

(a)            with the mutual written consent of Seller and Buyer; or

 

(b)            by either Seller or Buyer, if the Closing shall not have occurred on or before May 9, 2027, (the “Termination Date”); provided, however, that if, as of the Termination Date, all conditions set forth in Article 8, other than the conditions set forth in Section 8.1(a) or Section 8.1(b) (to the extent related to Section 8.1(a)), shall have been satisfied or shall be capable of being satisfied on the Closing Date were the Closing to occur at such time, or to the extent not prohibited by applicable Law shall have been waived on or before such date, either Buyer or Seller may extend the Termination Date by up to three (3) months, and such date thereafter shall be deemed to be the Termination Date; provided, further, that the right to terminate this Agreement under this Section 9.1(b) shall not be available to (i) any Party whose (or whose Affiliates) failure to perform any obligation or satisfy any condition under this Agreement has been the cause of, resulted in or contributed to the failure of the Closing to occur on or before the Termination Date (as it may be extended pursuant to this Section 9.1(b)) or (ii) Buyer if the Companies or Seller are pursuing an action that seeks to specifically enforce the performance of Buyer’s obligations under this Agreement to consummate the Closing or other similar equitable relief; or

 

(c)            by Seller, if Buyer shall have breached or failed to perform in any material respect any of its covenants in this Agreement, which breach or failure to perform (i) would give rise to the failure of a condition set forth in Section 8.1 or Section 8.3 being satisfied and (ii) (A) if capable of being cured, has not been cured by Buyer within thirty (30) days after its receipt of written notice thereof from Seller (provided that if Seller delivers such written notice within thirty (30) days of the Termination Date, the Termination Date shall automatically be extended to a date that is thirty (30) days after the date of such delivery); or (B) is incapable of being cured provided, however, that the right to terminate this Agreement pursuant to this Section 9.1(c) shall not be available to Seller if the failure of any such condition was caused (or materially contributed to) by Seller or the Companies; or

 

124 

 

 

(d)            by Buyer, if Seller shall have breached or failed to perform in any material respect any of its covenants in this Agreement, which breach or failure to perform (i) would give rise to the failure of a condition set forth in Section 8.1 or Section 8.2 being satisfied and (ii) (A) if capable of being cured, has not been cured by Seller within thirty (30) days after their receipt of written notice thereof from Buyer (provided that if Buyer delivers such written notice within thirty (30) days of the Termination Date, the Termination Date shall automatically be extended to a date that is thirty (30) days after the date of such delivery); or (B) is incapable of being cured; provided, however, that the right to terminate this Agreement pursuant to this Section 9.1(d) shall not be available to Buyer if the failure of any such condition was caused (or materially contributed to) by Buyer; or

 

(e)            by Seller if (i) all of the conditions set forth in Section 8.1 and Section 8.2 have been and continue to be satisfied or shall be capable of being satisfied at the Closing Date were the Closing to occur at such time and (ii) Buyer fails to consummate the transactions contemplated by this Agreement within two (2) Business Days of the date that the Closing should otherwise have occurred pursuant to Section 2.2 hereof and (iii) Seller and the Companies stood ready and willing to consummate the Closing on the date the Closing should otherwise have occurred pursuant to Section 2.2;

 

(f)             by either Seller or Buyer, if any Governmental Authority shall have issued a Governmental Order that permanently restrains, enjoins or otherwise prohibits the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, and such Governmental Order shall have become final and non-appealable; provided, however, that the right to terminate this Agreement under this Section 9.1(f) shall not be available to any Party whose (or whose Affiliate’s) failure to comply with Section 6.4(a) or Section 6.6 has caused, resulted in or contributed to, such action or inaction; or

 

(g)            by Seller, if a Buyer Material Adverse Effect under clause (a) of the definition thereof shall have occurred; provided, however, that the right to terminate this Agreement under this Section 9.1(g) shall not be available to Seller, with respect to any specific occurrence of a Buyer Material Adverse Effect under clause (a) of the definition thereof, following the date that is five (5) Business Days after the date on which Buyer notifies Seller in writing that such specific Buyer Material Adverse Effect under clause (a) of the definition thereof has occurred.

 

9.2            Effect of Termination. In the event of termination of this Agreement by either (a) Seller or (b) Buyer as provided in Section 9.1, this Agreement shall become void and have no further force or effect, without any Liability on the part of Buyer, Seller or the Companies; provided, however, that the provisions of Section 6.1(b), Section 7.3, this Section 9.2 and ARTICLE 11 will survive any termination hereof; provided, further, that nothing in this Section 9.2 shall relieve Seller, the Companies or Buyer of any Liability for any Willful Breach prior to termination. The Confidentiality Agreement will survive any termination hereof in accordance with its terms.

 

9.3            Notice of Termination. In order to validly terminate this Agreement pursuant to Section 9.1, the Party seeking to terminate this Agreement shall deliver written notice to the other Parties, specifying (i) the subsection of Section 9.1 pursuant to which this Agreement is being terminated and (ii) the factual basis, in reasonable detail, giving rise to such Party’s right to terminate this Agreement pursuant to Section 9.1.

ARTICLE 10

 

Survival; Indemnification

 

10.1          Indemnification.

 

(a)            Subject to the terms and conditions set forth in this Agreement, after the Closing, Seller shall indemnify and hold harmless Buyer and its Affiliates (each, a “Buyer Indemnified Party,” and collectively, the “Buyer Indemnified Parties”) from and against: any and all Losses actually incurred, suffered or sustained by such Buyer Indemnified Parties or any of them resulting from or arising out of (i) any breach of any representation or warranty made by Seller set forth in ARTICLE 3 or by the Companies set forth in ARTICLE 4, in each case on and as of the date hereof or on and as of the Closing, as if made as of the Closing (except that the accuracy of representations and warranties that by their terms speak as of a specified date will be determined as of such date), (ii) any breach of a covenant in this Agreement that is required to be performed by Seller following the Closing, (iii) any Liability for Seller Group Taxes, (iv) Fraud committed by a Company in the making of the representations and warranties in Article 4.

 

(b)           Subject to the terms and conditions set forth in this Agreement, after the Closing, Buyer shall indemnify and hold harmless Seller and its Affiliates (each, a “Seller Indemnified Party,” and collectively, the “Seller Indemnified Parties”) from and against any and all Losses incurred, suffered or sustained by such Seller Indemnified Parties or any of them resulting from or arising out of: (i) any breach of any representation or warranty made by Buyer set forth in ARTICLE 5, in each case on and as of the date hereof or on and as of the Closing, as if made as of the Closing (except that the accuracy of representations and warranties that by their terms speak as of a specified date will be determined as of such date); (ii) any breach of a covenant in this Agreement that is required to be performed by Buyer or any Company following the Closing and (iii) the Offset Obligation and/or the Offset Project.

 

10.2         Survival of Representations; Warranties and Covenants.

 

(a)            The representations, warranties, covenants and agreements contained in this Agreement or any certificate delivered pursuant hereto shall survive and expire as follows:

 

(i)             each representation and warranty of Seller in ARTICLE 3, each representation and warranty of the Companies set forth in ARTICLE 4 and each representation and warranty of Buyer set forth in ARTICLE 5 of this Agreement will survive for a period of eighteen (18) months following the Closing Date (such date, the “Representations Expiration Date”); and immediately upon the Representations Expiration Date, the applicable representations and warranties will immediately expire and terminate and no Person shall have any Liability resulting from or arising out of the foregoing;

 

(ii)            each covenant and agreement of a Party contained in this Agreement or in any certificate delivered pursuant hereto, in each case, that is required to be performed or that contemplates performance prior to the Closing shall not survive the Closing and shall immediately expire and terminate at the Closing (the “Pre-Closing Covenant Expiration Time”), and immediately upon the Pre-Closing Covenant Expiration Time, such covenants will immediately expire and terminate and no Person shall have any Liability resulting from or arising out of the foregoing; and

 

126 

 

  

(iii)           each covenant and agreement of a Party hereto contained in this Agreement or in any certificate delivered pursuant hereto, to the extent that such covenant or agreement obligates a Party hereto to perform following the Closing such covenant or agreement, shall survive in accordance with the terms provided hereunder or, if no term is provided until the date of full performance or the date that is twelve (12) months following the Closing, whichever is later (such date, the “Post-Closing Covenant Expiration Date”), and immediately upon the Post-Closing Covenant Expiration Date, the applicable covenants and agreements will immediately expire and terminate and no Person shall have any Liability resulting from or arising out of the foregoing.

 

(iv)          the matters set forth in Section 10.1(b)(iii) shall survive until such time as the Offset Release is obtained and delivered to Seller (the “Offset Expiration Time”).

 

(v)           the matters set forth in Sections 10.1(a)(iii) and (iv) shall survive until the expiration of the applicable statute of limitations plus thirty (30) days (such date, the “Tax & Fraud Indemnity Expiration Date” and, together with the Representations Expiration Date, the Pre-Closing Covenant Expiration Time, the Post-Closing Covenant Expiration Date and the Offset Expiration Time, each, an “Expiration Date”).

 

(b)           Notwithstanding anything to the contrary contained herein, no Claim, Losses or Liability may be asserted following the expiration of the Expiration Date period in respect of the representation, warranty, covenant or agreement that is the basis for such Claim; provided, however, that if, at any time prior to such Expiration Date, an Indemnified Party properly delivers to the applicable Indemnifying Party a written Claim Notice in accordance with this ARTICLE 10, then the Claim asserted in such notice, and the indemnification obligation of the Indemnifying Party with respect to such Claim, shall survive the applicable Expiration Date until such Claim is fully and finally resolved in accordance with the terms of this Agreement. To the extent permitted by Law, it is the intention of the parties hereto that the statute of limitations be modified as described above.

 

10.3         Limitations.

 

(a)            Limitations on Losses. Notwithstanding anything to the contrary contained herein, the amount of any Losses for which Seller, in its capacity as an Indemnifying Party, or Buyer, in its capacity as an Indemnifying Party, otherwise would have an indemnification obligation under this Article shall not include (i) punitive, incidental, indirect, special or consequential damages, except, in each case, to the extent awarded to a Third Party by a court of competent jurisdiction, in a final, non-appealable decision in a Third Party Claim asserted by such Third Party, (ii) any Losses that are calculated as a multiple of any financial measure, diminution in value or lost profits or revenue or other economic benefit or (iii) any non-monetary Liability.

 

127 

 

 

(b)           Deductible and Cap Amounts. Notwithstanding anything to the contrary set forth in this Agreement, Seller shall not have any obligation to indemnify any Buyer Indemnified Party pursuant to Section 10.1(a)(i): (i) for any specific matter (or series of related matters arising out of the same facts) until the aggregate amount of all such Losses for which such Seller would otherwise have an indemnification obligation exceeds $[***] (the “Mini-Basket”), and in such case, Seller shall, subject to the other limitations and agreements set forth in this Agreement, be responsible for all such Losses including the Mini-Basket; provided, however, if the Losses in respect of such matter (or series of related matters arising out of the same facts) do not exceed the Mini-Basket in the aggregate, then such Losses shall be excluded from the calculation of the Deductible; (ii) subject to the immediately preceding clause (i), unless and until the aggregate Losses incurred or suffered by all Buyer Indemnified Parties (or any of them) exceed $[***] (the “Deductible”), in which case, Seller shall be responsible only for such Losses to the extent they exceed the Deductible and not for any Losses that are less than or equal to the Deductible; and (iii) after the aggregate Losses paid to Buyer Indemnified Parties (or any of them) by Seller exceed, in the aggregate, $[***] (the “Cap”); provided, that the Cap shall not apply to any amounts payable for Losses resulting or arising from Fraud. Buyer shall have no obligation to indemnify any Seller Indemnified Parties pursuant to Section 10.1(b)(i) (i) for any specific matter (or series of related matters arising out of the same facts) until the aggregate amount of all such Losses for which Buyer would otherwise have an indemnification obligation exceeds the Mini-Basket, and in such case, Buyer shall be responsible for all such Losses including the Mini-Basket and (ii) after the aggregate Losses paid to Seller Indemnified Parties (or any of them) by Buyer exceed the Cap; provided, that the Cap shall not apply to any amounts payable for Losses resulting or arising from Fraud or with respect to any breach of any representation or warranty of Buyer set forth in Sections 5.1, 5.2, 5.3, 5.4(a)(i), 5.4(a)(ii), 5.8, 5.10 and 5.11. Notwithstanding anything to the contrary contained in this Agreement, the cumulative Liability of (1) Buyer, the Companies (from and after the Closing) or any of their respective Affiliates (or any of them) arising out of or related to this Agreement and the transactions contemplated hereby shall not exceed the Dollar value of the Aggregate Consideration, and (2) Seller and the Companies (prior to the Closing) and their respective Affiliates (or any of them) arising out of or related to this Agreement and the transactions contemplated hereby shall not exceed the Dollar value of the Aggregate Consideration actually issued to and received by Seller from Buyer hereunder, including, for the avoidance of doubt with respect to any Liabilities in respect of, resulting from or arising out of (x) Fraud or (y) any breach of a covenant in this Agreement.

 

(c)            Additional Limitations.

 

(i)             Notwithstanding anything to the contrary set forth in this Agreement, the amount of Losses for which an Indemnifying Party otherwise would have any Liability arising out of this Agreement shall be determined net of: (A) any amounts (other than proceeds from self-insurance or similar policies) that the Indemnified Party or any of its Affiliates has actually received from any Third Party insurer in respect of the Claim relating to the Losses sought (net of any deductible, retention, retropremiums and costs of recovery incurred by the Indemnified Party or any of its Affiliates) prior to the date that the indemnification payment in respect of the Claim relating to the Losses sought is required under this Agreement or by a judgment as may be rendered by a court referred to in Section 11.9 to be paid by the Indemnifying Party; (B) any amounts from any Third Party that the Indemnified Party or any of its Affiliates has actually received in respect of the Claim relating to the Losses sought (net of the cost of recovery thereof incurred by the Indemnified Party or any of its Affiliates) prior to the date that the payment for such Losses is required under this Agreement or by a judgment as may be rendered by a court referred to in Section 11.9 in a final, non-appealable decision to be paid by the Indemnifying Party and (C) any Tax savings or benefits actually realized by any Buyer Indemnified Party or its Affiliates through the reduction of cash Taxes payable in the Taxable year of the Loss or the succeeding Taxable year that is attributable to any deduction, loss, credit or other Tax benefit resulting from or arising out of such Losses.

 

128 

 

 

(ii)            For purposes of calculating the amount of any Losses arising out of any inaccuracy or breach of any representation or warranty in this Agreement, the representations and warranties will be read without regard to any materiality or Material Adverse Effect qualifiers contained in such representations and warranties; provided, however, that the full effect shall be given to such qualifiers for purposes of determining the existence of a breach of any such representations and warranties.

 

(iii)           Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in this Agreement, no Buyer Indemnified Party shall be entitled to indemnification for any Losses attributable to Taxes: (A) resulting from transactions or actions taken by Buyer, the Company or any of their respective Affiliates on the Closing Date, after the Closing, to the extent outside of the ordinary course of business, (B) imposed with respect to any Taxable period (or portion thereof) beginning after the Closing Date, (C) to the extent attributable to Buyer’s breach of any of the Tax covenants contained herein, or (D) to the extent taken into account in the determination of the Closing Adjustment Amount, the Aggregate Consideration (including the value thereof) or any of the components of any of the foregoing.

 

10.4         Claim Procedure.

 

(a)            Claim Notice. The obligations of an Indemnifying Party under this ARTICLE 10 in connection with any Claim by an Indemnified Party, other than in respect of a Third Party Claim, are subject to the following terms and conditions. The Indemnified Party shall, as promptly as practicable upon becoming aware of the facts, matters or circumstances giving rise to a Claim for indemnification, deliver to the Indemnifying Party notice of such Claim (each such notice, a “Claim Notice”) stating (to the extent known or reasonably anticipated) a good faith description of the nature and basis of such Claim and, to the extent reasonably available to the Indemnified Party, the estimated amount of any Loss in respect thereof (the “Claimed Amount”); provided, however, that, the failure to timely deliver such Claim Notice shall not affect the rights of the Indemnified Party hereunder except to the extent (and then only to the extent) that the Indemnifying Party shall have been materially prejudiced as a result of such failure.

 

(b)           Response to a Notice of Claim. Within 30 days after receipt of a Claim Notice, the Indemnifying Party shall deliver to the Indemnified Party a written response (the “Response”) in which the Indemnifying Party shall either:

 

(i)           agree that the Indemnified Party is entitled to receive all of the Claimed Amount;

 

129 

 

 

(ii)          agree that the Indemnified Party is entitled to receive part, but not all, of the Claimed Amount (such portion, the “Agreed Portion”); or

 

(iii)        dispute any or all of the Claims that the Indemnified Party has asserted, including whether the Indemnified Party is entitled to receive any or all of the Claimed Amount, and describe in reasonable detail (A) its objection to such Claim Notice or Claimed Amount, and (B) the nature and basis of such objection.

 

If the Indemnifying Party does not provide a Response within such thirty (30) day period following its receipt of such Claim Notice, the Indemnifying Party will be deemed to have objected in full to the Claims or the Claimed Amounts contemplated by the Claim Notice.

 

(c)            Contested Claims. In the event that the Indemnifying Party disputes or is deemed to have objected to the Claims set forth in the Claim Notice or the Claimed Amount (or the portion of the Claims set forth in the Claim Notice or the Claimed Amount not comprising the Agreed Portion) pursuant to Section 10.4(b), the Indemnifying Party shall not be obligated to indemnify the Indemnified Party for the disputed or deemed disputed portion until either (i) the Indemnified Party and the Indemnifying Party mutually agree in a written and binding agreement as to the disputed Claims set forth in the Claim Notice and the Claimed Amount (or any other amount mutually agreed upon by such Persons), in which case the Indemnifying Party shall pay the agreed amount within five (5) Business Days of such agreement, or (ii) a final, non-appealable Governmental Order has been entered (as may be rendered by a court referred to in Section 11.9) resolving the Claims contested in such Response or deemed objected to and ordering the Indemnifying Party to pay such Claimed Amount (or portion thereof) to the Indemnified Party, in which case the Indemnifying Party shall pay such amount within five (5) Business Days of such Governmental Order becoming final and non-appealable.

 

(d)           Third Party Claims.

 

(i)          The indemnity obligations of the Indemnifying Parties under this ARTICLE 10 that result from any Third Party Claim shall be subject to the following terms and conditions. If any Third Party shall notify the Indemnified Party of any Claim (a “Third Party Claim”) for which such Indemnified Party wishes to seek indemnification pursuant to this ARTICLE 10, then the Indemnified Party shall as promptly as practicable upon receipt of such notification deliver a Claim Notice to the Indemnifying Party of such Third Party Claim; provided, that the Indemnified Party’s failure to do so shall not relieve the Indemnifying Party of its indemnification obligations hereunder, except to the extent and only to the extent the Indemnifying Party is materially prejudiced by the failure to timely deliver such Claim Notice. Thereafter, the Indemnified Party shall promptly deliver to the Indemnifying Party copies of all material correspondence with such Third Party, its Affiliates or their respective Associated Persons or filings with a court received by the Indemnified Party relating to the Third Party Claim, except for the portions of any such filings that are subject to work product, attorney-client or other privileges. If the Indemnifying Party, (A) agrees in writing to assume responsibility for all Losses arising out of such Third Party Claim, and (B) reasonably demonstrates to the Indemnified Party in writing the financial ability of the Indemnifying Party to provide such indemnification with respect to such Third Party Claim, then the Indemnifying Party shall have the right to undertake, by counsel of its own choosing, the defense of such Third Party Claim at the Indemnifying Party’s sole risk and expense; provided, however, that, notwithstanding the foregoing, the Indemnified Party shall have the sole right to control the defense, compromise and settlement of any Third Party Claim (in each case, subject to the limitations set forth in this ARTICLE 10), to the extent that (I) such Third Party Claim involves or would result in criminal Liability or admission of criminal wrongdoing by the Indemnified Party or any of its Affiliates, (II) such Third Party Claim seeks injunctive or other equitable relief against the Indemnified Party or any of its Affiliates, (III) such Third Party Claim involves or would result in a finding or admission of a violation of Law by the Indemnified Party or any of its Affiliates or (IV) to the extent, with respect to any claim for indemnification under Section 10.1(a)(i) or Section 10.1(b)(i), such Third Party Claim would result in Losses in excess of the Cap (each, a “ Controlled Claim”). In the case of any Controlled Claim, the Indemnified Party shall keep the Indemnifying Party reasonably informed of the status and progress of such Third Party Claim and shall consider in good faith any recommendations of the Indemnifying Party with respect to the defense of such Third Party Claim.

 

130 

 

 

(ii)         Notwithstanding anything in this ARTICLE 10 to the contrary, the Indemnifying Party shall not, and shall cause its Affiliates and its and their respective Representatives not to, without the Indemnified Party’s prior written consent (which consent shall not be unreasonably withheld, delayed, or conditioned), settle or compromise any Third Party Claim or consent to the entry of any Governmental Order, unless (A) such settlement, compromise or Governmental Order includes as an unconditional term thereof the Indemnified Party receiving from the Third Party an irrevocable release from all Liability in respect of such Third Party Claim, in form and substance reasonably satisfactory to the Indemnified Party, (B) such settlement, compromise or Governmental Order would not result in the finding or admission of any violation of Law, and (C) such settlement, compromise or Governmental Order does not impose any injunctive relief or operational restrictions on the Indemnified Party.

 

(iii)        Notwithstanding anything in this ARTICLE 10 to the contrary, the Indemnified Party shall not, and shall cause its Affiliates and its and their respective Representatives not to, without the Indemnifying Party’s prior written consent (which consent shall not be unreasonably withheld, delayed, or conditioned), settle or compromise any Third Party Claim or consent to the entry of any Governmental Order with respect to any Third Party Claim unless (A) such settlement, compromise or order would not result in the finding or admission of any violation of Law, or the incurrence of any Liability, in each case, by the Indemnifying Person or any of its Affiliates (regardless of whether or not the Indemnifying Person agreed to assume responsibility for all Losses arising out of such Third Party Claim pursuant to Section 10.4(d)(i)(A)), and (B) such settlement, compromise or Governmental Order does not impose any injunctive relief or operational restrictions on the Indemnifying Person.

 

10.5         Exclusive Remedy; Order of Recourse.

 

(a)            Except (i) with respect to Fraud, (ii) as permitted by Section 2.4, (iii) pursuant to Section 9.2, or (iv) for non-monetary equitable relief pursuant to Section 11.11, indemnification pursuant to this ARTICLE 10 shall be the sole and exclusive remedy for the Parties for any Claim or Liability in respect of this Agreement or the transactions contemplated hereby, including for (A) any breach of or inaccuracy in any representation or warranty, (B) breach of any covenant, agreement or other obligation contained in this Agreement or (C) breach of or inaccuracy in any certificate delivered under this Agreement; provided, that nothing herein shall restrict Buyer from recovery against the R&W Insurance Policy.

 

131 

 

 

(b)           In connection with any Claim for indemnification by a Buyer Indemnified Party under Section 10.1(a), Buyer shall, and shall cause each Buyer Indemnified Party to, seek payment for all amounts in respect of such Claim, and all amounts, if any, payable in respect of such Claim shall, subject to the other limitations set forth in this Agreement, be satisfied (i) first, from the R&W Insurance Policy for so long as and to the extent that, in each case, the R&W Insurance Policy is Available (an “Insurable Claim”) and (ii) second, to the extent that the R&W Insurance Policy is not Available, from Seller. Without limiting any other rights, remedies and/or defenses of Seller in connection with the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, Seller shall not be liable for any Losses in respect of any Claim by a Buyer Indemnified Party under this Agreement for so long as and to the extent that such Claim is an Insurable Claim. For the purposes of determining whether the R&W Insurance Policy is “Available” hereunder with respect to a Claim, the R&W Insurance Policy shall be deemed Available at all times except to the extent that (A) the amount of the Loss for such Claim exceeds the R&W Insurance Policy limits, or (B) the insurer under the R&W Insurance Policy has provided a final denial of coverage for such Claim.

11.5         Successors and Assigns; Assignment; Buyer Designated Affiliate.

(b)           Notwithstanding the foregoing, Buyer shall be entitled to designate one or more of its Affiliates that are directly or indirectly wholly owned by Buyer (each, a “Buyer Designated Affiliate”) to be the purchaser of transferee of some or all of the Purchased Interests (and to be a counterparty to one or more of the Related Agreements); provided that no such designation (i) shall release Buyer from its obligations under this Agreement or the Related Agreements or (ii) would be reasonably expected to restrict or delay the consummation of the transactions contemplated by this Agreement or the Related Agreements. Buyer shall be responsible for and shall pay all or reimburse Seller for any incremental Tax liabilities and other out-of-pocket costs and expenses resulting from the substitution of a Buyer Designated Affiliate for Buyer as the purchaser or transferee of any Purchased Interests. In furtherance of the foregoing, at the Closing, where Buyer has designated a Buyer Designated Affiliate as the purchaser or transferee of Transferred Interests, Buyer shall execute and deliver to Seller a Buyer Guarantee which, among other things, guarantees the liabilities and obligations of such Buyer Designated Affiliate.

11.8         Applicable Law. This Agreement, and all claims or causes of action (whether in contract, tort or statute) that may be based upon, arise out of or relate to this Agreement, or the negotiation, execution or performance of this Agreement (including any claim or cause of action based upon, arising out of or related to any representation or warranty made in or in connection with this Agreement or as an inducement to enter into this Agreement), shall be governed by and enforced in accordance with the internal Laws of the State of Delaware, without giving effect to any Laws, rules or provisions of the State of Delaware that would cause the application of the Laws, rules or provisions of any jurisdiction other than the State of Delaware.

 

11.9         Jurisdiction of Disputes. In the event any Party to this Agreement commences any litigation, proceeding or other legal action in connection with or relating to this Agreement, any Related Agreement or any matters described or contemplated herein or therein, the Parties hereby (a) agree that any litigation, proceeding or other legal action shall be instituted exclusively in the Delaware Court of Chancery in and for New Castle County, or in the event (and only in the event) that such Delaware Court of Chancery does not have subject matter jurisdiction over such dispute, any Delaware State court sitting in New Castle County, unless the federal courts have exclusive jurisdiction, in which case the federal courts located in New Castle County in the State of Delaware (such courts, including appellate courts therefrom, the “Specified Courts”); (b) agree that in the event of any such litigation, proceeding or action, the Parties will consent and submit to personal jurisdiction in the applicable Specified Court and to service of process upon them in accordance with the rules and statutes governing service of process (it being understood that nothing in this Section 11.9 shall be deemed to prevent any Party from seeking to remove any action to a United States federal court that is a Specified Court); (c) agree to waive to the full extent permitted by law any objection that they may now or hereafter have to the venue of any such litigation, proceeding or action in any such court or that any such litigation, proceeding or action was brought in an inconvenient forum; (d) agree as an alternative method of service to service of process in any legal proceeding by mailing of copies thereof to such party at its address set forth in Section 11.2 for communications to such Party; (e) agree that any service made as provided herein shall be effective and binding service in every respect; and (f) agree that nothing herein shall affect the rights of any Party to effect service of process in any other manner permitted by Law; provided, however, that if the Specified Courts are functionally unavailable as a result of any Public Health Event, each Party agrees to submit to the jurisdiction of any state in the United States in which the federal court therein may assert jurisdiction over the Parties solely in respect of applications for temporary, status quo or interim injunctive relief.

 

135 

 

 

11.10       Waiver of Jury Trial. Each Party acknowledges and agrees that any controversy that may arise under this Agreement or the Related Agreements is likely to involve complicated and difficult issues, and therefore each such Party hereby irrevocably and unconditionally waives any right such party may have to a trial by jury in respect of any litigation directly or indirectly arising out of or relating to this Agreement or the Related Agreements, or the transactions contemplated hereby or thereby. Each Party certifies and acknowledges that (a) no representative, agent or attorney of any other Party has represented, expressly or otherwise, that such other Party would not, in the event of litigation, seek to enforce the foregoing waiver; (b) each Party understands and has considered the implications of this waiver; (c) each Party makes this waiver voluntarily; and (d) each Party has been induced to enter into this Agreement by, among other things, the mutual waivers and certifications in this Section 11.10.

 

11.11       Specific Performance.

 

(a)            Subject to the terms of this Agreement, Seller and the Companies agree that Buyer shall have the right, in addition to any other rights and remedies existing in its favor, to enforce its rights and the obligations hereunder not only by an action or actions for damages but also by an action or actions for specific performance, injunctive and/or other equitable relief. Furthermore, subject to the terms of this Agreement, Buyer agrees that Seller and the Companies shall have the right, in addition to any other rights and remedies existing in its favor, to enforce its rights and the obligations of Buyer hereunder not only by an action or actions for damages but also by an action or actions for specific performance, injunctive and/or other equitable relief.

 

(b)            The right of specific performance and other equitable relief is an integral part of the transactions contemplated by this Agreement and without that right, none of the Parties would have entered into this Agreement. The Parties agree not to assert that a remedy of specific performance or other equitable relief is unenforceable, invalid, contrary to law or inequitable for any reason, and not to assert that a remedy of monetary damages would provide an adequate remedy or that the Parties otherwise have an adequate remedy at law. The Parties acknowledge and agree that any Party seeking an injunction or injunctions to prevent breaches of this Agreement and to enforce specifically the terms and provisions of this Agreement in accordance with this Section 11.11 shall not be required to provide any bond or other security in connection with any such order or injunction.

 

136 

 

 

(c)            If, prior to the Termination Date, any Party brings any action, in each case in accordance with this Section 11.11, to enforce specifically the performance of the terms and provisions hereof by any other Party, the Termination Date shall automatically be extended (i) for the period during which such action is pending, plus ten (10) Business Days or (ii) by such other time period established by the court presiding over such action, as the case may be.

 

(d)            Notwithstanding anything herein to the contrary, in no event shall this Section 11.11 be used, alone or together with any other provision of this Agreement, to require Seller or the Companies to remedy any breach of any representation or warranty of Seller or the Companies made herein.

 

11.12       Severability. Any term or provision of this Agreement that is invalid or unenforceable in any situation in any jurisdiction shall not affect the validity or enforceability of the remaining terms and provisions hereof or the validity or enforceability of the offending term or provision in any other situation or in any other jurisdiction. If the final judgment of a court of competent jurisdiction declares that any term or provision hereof is invalid or unenforceable, the Parties agree that the court making the determination of invalidity or unenforceability shall have the power to reduce the scope, duration, or area of the term or provision, to delete specific words or phrases, or to replace any invalid or unenforceable term or provision with a term or provision that is valid and enforceable and that comes closest to expressing the intention of the invalid or unenforceable term or provision, and this Agreement shall be enforceable as so modified after the expiration of the time within which the judgment may be appealed.

 

11.13       Construction. The Parties have participated jointly in the negotiation and drafting of this Agreement. In the event an ambiguity or question of intent or interpretation arises, the language shall be construed as mutually chosen by the Parties to express their mutual intent, and no rule of strict construction shall be applied against any Party. The Parties acknowledge and agree that they are sophisticated parties, have reviewed the terms of this Agreement and the Related Agreements, engaged counsel and advisors as they have each determined necessary to fully understand their respective rights and obligations hereunder and thereunder, and accordingly, no agreement, provision, condition, waiver, representation, warranty, acknowledgement or other term hereof or thereof shall be deemed unenforceable or otherwise inoperable for lack of conspicuousness or emphatic text.

 

11.14       Counterparts. This Agreement may be executed in counterparts (including using any electronic signature covered by the United States ESIGN Act of 2000, Uniform Electronic Transactions Act, the Electronic Signatures and Records Act or other applicable law, e.g., www.docusign.com), and such counterparts may be delivered in electronic format, including by facsimile, email or other transmission method. Such delivery of counterparts shall be conclusive evidence of the intent to be bound hereby and each such counterpart, including those delivered in electronic format, and copies produced therefrom shall have the same effect as an originally signed counterpart. To the extent applicable, the foregoing constitutes the election of the Parties to invoke any Law authorizing electronic signatures. Minor variations in the form of the signature page, including footers from earlier versions of this Agreement, shall be disregarded in determining a Party’s intent or the effectiveness of such signature. No Party shall raise the use the delivery of signatures to this Agreement in electronic format as a defense to the formation of a contract and each such Party forever waives any such defense.

 

137 

 

 

11.15        Retention of Advisors. Each of the Parties acknowledges and agrees that Seller Group Counsel has represented Seller and the Companies in connection with the negotiation, preparation, execution, delivery and performance of this Agreement and the Related Agreements and the consummation of the transactions contemplated hereby and thereby, and that Seller, the Companies and their respective Associated Persons (each, a “Seller Group Member”) have a reasonable expectation that Seller Group Counsel will represent them in connection with any claim or Action involving any Seller Group Member, on the one hand, and Buyer or any of its Associated Persons (each, a “Buyer Group Member”), on the other hand, arising under this Agreement, the Related Agreements or the transactions contemplated hereby and thereby. Buyer hereby, on behalf of itself and the other Buyer Group Members and their respective successors and assigns, hereby irrevocably (a) agrees to any such representation in any such matter and (b) waives any actual or potential conflict arising from any such representation in the event of: (i) any adversity between the interests of any Seller Group Member, on the one hand, and Buyer and the Companies, on the other hand, in any such matter; and/or (ii) any communication between or among Seller Group Counsel, the Companies and their respective Affiliates or employees, whether privileged or not, or any other information known to such counsel, by reason of such counsel’s representation of the Companies prior to Closing.

 

11.16       Protected Communication.

 

(a)            The Parties to this Agreement agree that, immediately prior to the Closing, without the need for any further action (i) all right, title and interest of the Companies in and to all Protected Communications shall thereupon transfer to and be vested solely in Seller and their successors in interest and (ii) any and all protections from disclosure, including attorney-client privileges and work product protections, associated with or arising from any Protected Communications that would have been exercisable by the Companies shall thereupon be vested exclusively in Seller and their successors in interest and shall be exercised or waived solely as directed by Seller or its successors in interest.

 

(b)            None of Buyer, the Companies or any Person acting on any of their behalf shall, without the prior written consent of Seller or its successors in interest, assert or waive or attempt to assert or waive any such protection against disclosure, including the attorney-client privilege or work product protection, or to access, discover, obtain, use or disclose or attempt to access, discover, obtain, use or disclose any Protected Communications in any manner, including in connection with any dispute or legal proceeding relating to or in connection with this Agreement, the events and negotiations leading to this Agreement, or any of the transactions contemplated hereby and by the Related Agreements; provided, however, (i) the foregoing shall neither prohibit Buyer from seeking proper discovery of such documents nor Seller from asserting that such documents are not discoverable to the extent that applicable attorney-client privileges and work product protections have attached thereto and (ii) in the event a dispute arises between any Buyer Group Members, on the one hand, and any other Person (other than the Seller Group Members), on the other hand, such Buyer Group Members shall not disclose any documents or information subject to protections from disclosure, including attorney-client privileges and work product protections, associated with or arising from any Protected Communications without the prior written consent of Seller (such consent not to be unreasonably withheld, conditioned or delayed) (provided that if such Buyer Group Members are required by judicial order or other legal process to make such disclosure, such Buyer Group Members shall promptly notify Seller in writing of such requirement (without making disclosure) and shall provide Seller with such cooperation and assistance as shall be necessary to enable Seller to prevent disclosure by reason of any protection against disclosure, including the attorney-client privileges and work product protections).

 

138 

 

 

(c)            Without limiting the generality of the foregoing, (i) Seller shall have the right to retain, or cause Seller Group Counsel to retain, any Protected Communications in possession of Seller Group Counsel at the Closing and (ii) Buyer shall (and following the Closing shall cause the Companies to) use commercially reasonable efforts to ensure that any and all protections from disclosure, including, attorney-client privileges and work product protections, associated with or arising from any Protected Communications will survive the Closing, remain in effect and transfer to and be vested solely in Seller and its successors in interest.

 

(d)           Seller and its successors in interest shall have the right at any time prior to or following the Closing to remove, erase, delete, disable, copy or otherwise deal with any Protected Communications in whatever way they desire, and Buyer and the Companies shall, at Seller’s expense, provide full access to all Protected Communications in their possession or within their direct or indirect control and shall provide reasonable assistance at the expense of the Person requesting such assistance in order to give full force and effect to the rights of Seller and its successors in interest hereunder.

 

(e)           This Section 11.16 is for the benefit of the Seller Group Members and such Persons are intended third-party beneficiaries.

 

11.17       No Waiver of Privilege, Protection from Disclosure or Use. The Parties understand and agree that nothing in this Agreement, including the provisions of Section 7.1, Section 11.15 and Section 11.16 regarding the assertions of protection from disclosure and use, privilege and conflicts of interest, shall be deemed to be a waiver of any applicable attorney-client privilege or other protection from disclosure or use. Each of the Parties understands and agrees that it has undertaken commercially reasonable efforts to prevent the disclosure of Protected Communications. Notwithstanding those efforts, the Parties understand and agree that the consummation of the transactions contemplated by this Agreement could result in the inadvertent disclosure of information that may be confidential, eligible to be subject to a claim of privilege, or otherwise protected from disclosure. The Parties further understand and agree that any disclosure of information that may be confidential, subject to a claim of privilege, or otherwise protected from disclosure will not constitute a waiver of or otherwise prejudice any claim of confidentiality, privilege, or protection from disclosure, including with respect to information involving or concerning the same subject matter as the disclosed information. The Parties agree to use commercially reasonable efforts to return any inadvertently disclosed information to the disclosing Party promptly upon becoming aware of its existence. The Parties further agree that promptly after the return of any inadvertently disclosed information, the Party returning such information shall destroy any and all copies, summaries, descriptions and/or notes of such inadvertently disclosed information, including electronic versions thereof, and all portions of larger documents or communications that contain such copies, summaries, descriptions and/or notes.

 

11.18       Relationship of the Parties. Nothing in this Agreement shall be deemed to constitute the Parties as joint venturers, alter egos, partners or participants in an unincorporated business or other separate entity, nor, except as expressly and specifically set forth in this Agreement in any manner create any principal-agent, fiduciary or other special relationship between the Parties. No Party shall have any duties (including fiduciary duties) towards any other Party except as specifically set forth herein.

 

139 

 

 

11.19        No Right of Set-Off. Buyer, for itself and its Affiliates and successors and permitted assigns, hereby unconditionally and irrevocably waives any rights of set-off, netting, offset, recoupment, or similar rights that Buyer or any of its Affiliates or successors and permitted assigns has or may have with respect to the payment of the Aggregate Consideration (and/or the value of the Aggregate Consideration), the delivery of the Warrants (and/or the value of the Warrants and/or the shares issuable thereunder) or any other payments to be made by Buyer or its Affiliates pursuant to this Agreement or any other document or instrument delivered by Buyer or its Affiliates in connection herewith.

 

11.20       Translation of Currencies. Except as otherwise expressly set forth herein, in the event that the Parties need to convert currencies under this Agreement, the relevant exchange rate shall be determined based on the rate that is in effect two (2) Business Days preceding the applicable determination date as published on Oanda.com.
